0),this.stats={buildings:a.buildings.renderedBuildings,streets:a.roads.sourceFeatures,terrainVertices:a.terrain.nx*a.terrain.nz+(a.terrain.northShorePatch?.vertices??0)},this.applyPalette(this.palette),this.freeFlightEnabled||this.setView(this.activeView,!1),this.onStatus({phase:"ready",progress:100,message:"City ready",stats:this.stats})}catch(e){console.error(e),this.onStatus({phase:"error",progress:100,message:"City data could not load. Showing a silhouette preview."})}}createCinematicTour() {
  const nodes = [];
  const pass = (id, offset) => {
    const target = this.viewPose(id).target.clone();
    const position = target.clone().add(new C(...offset));
    position.y = Math.max(position.y, 480);
    nodes.push({ id, target, position });
  };
  nodes.push({ id: 'overview', position: new C(-2800, 1650, 5200), target: new C(-800, 130, -600) });
  pass('city_hall', [-650, 530, 850]);
  pass('city_hall', [700, 490, 420]);
  pass('downtown', [-1150, 600, 1300]);
  pass('salesforce_tower', [740, 400, 650]);
  pass('salesforce_tower', [760, 400, -430]);
  pass('ferry_building', [800, 490, 530]);
  pass('ferry_building', [850, 500, -460]);
  pass('transamerica_pyramid', [700, 460, -480]);
  pass('transamerica_pyramid', [-680, 460, -570]);
  pass('coit_tower', [670, 500, -460]);
  pass('coit_tower', [-700, 540, -750]);
  pass('golden_gate', [1600, 650, 1450]);
  pass('golden_gate', [1050, 460, -1250]);
  nodes.push({ id: 'overview', position: new C(-6300, 1250, -650), target: new C(-1900, 130, -900) });
  nodes.push({ id: 'overview', position: new C(-5200, 1600, 3400), target: new C(-1000, 130, -500) });
  let total = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
    nodes[i].duration = We.clamp(nodes[i].position.distanceTo(nodes[(i + 1) % nodes.length].position) / 210, 4.5, 15);
    total += nodes[i].duration;
  }
  let start = 0;
  for (const node of nodes) {
    node.duration *= 120 / total;
    node.start = start; start += node.duration;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
    const previous = nodes[(i + nodes.length - 1) % nodes.length];
    const next = nodes[(i + 1) % nodes.length];
    const span = previous.duration + nodes[i].duration;
    nodes[i].velocity = next.position.clone().sub(previous.position).multiplyScalar(1 / span);
    nodes[i].targetVelocity = next.target.clone().sub(previous.target).multiplyScalar(1 / span);
  }
  this.tourPaletteCache = Object.fromEntries(['day', 'dusk', 'night'].map(name => [name, this.captureTourPalette(name)]));
  return { nodes, duration: 120, elapsed: 0, playing: true,
    entrancePosition: this.camera.position.clone(), entranceTarget: this.target.clone(),
    entranceFov: this.camera.fov, entrancePalette: this.captureTourPalette(),
    position: new C(), target: new C() };
}
captureTourPalette(name) {
  const palette = name && I3[name];
  const colors = {}, floats = {}, sky = {};
  for (const [key, source] of Object.entries({uShadeHue:'shadeHue',uFogColor:'fog',uInk:'ink'})) {
    colors[key] = palette ? new Ue(palette[source]) : this.globalUniforms[key].value.clone();
  }
  for (const [key, source] of Object.entries({uTerminator:'terminator',uFogNear:'fogNear',uFogFar:'fogFar',uFogMax:'fogMax'})) {
    floats[key] = palette ? palette[source] : this.globalUniforms[key].value;
  }
  floats.uShadeMix = palette ? (name === 'night' ? .64 : name === 'dusk' ? .55 : .5) : this.globalUniforms.uShadeMix.value;
  floats.uShadeDark = palette ? (name === 'night' ? .85 : name === 'dusk' ? .9 : .93) : this.globalUniforms.uShadeDark.value;
  for (const [key, source] of Object.entries({uZenith:'zenith',uHorizon:'horizon',uSunColor:'sun'})) {
    sky[key] = palette ? new Ue(palette[source]) : this.skyMaterial.uniforms[key].value.clone();
  }
  const generic = this.genericMaterials[0]?.uniforms;
  return { colors, floats, sky,
    sunDir: palette ? new C(...palette.sunDir).normalize() : this.globalUniforms.uSunDir.value.clone(),
    low: palette ? new Ue(palette.low) : (generic?.uLow.value.clone() ?? new Ue(I3.day.low)),
    high: palette ? new Ue(palette.high) : (generic?.uHigh.value.clone() ?? new Ue(I3.day.high)),
    stars: palette ? palette.stars : this.skyMaterial.uniforms.uStars.value,
    materials: this.materialRecords.map(record => palette ? this.colorForRole(record.role, record.fallback, palette) : record.material.uniforms.uLit.value.clone()) };
}
applyTourPalette(from, to, mix, entrance, entranceMix = 1) {
  const a = typeof from === 'string' ? this.tourPaletteCache[from] : from;
  const b = typeof to === 'string' ? this.tourPaletteCache[to] : to;
  const blend = (value, first, second, initial) => {
    value.copy(first).lerp(second, mix);
    if (entrance && entranceMix < 1) value.lerp(initial, 1 - entranceMix);
  };
  for (const key of Object.keys(a.colors)) blend(this.globalUniforms[key].value, a.colors[key], b.colors[key], entrance?.colors[key]);
  for (const key of Object.keys(a.floats)) {
    const value = We.lerp(a.floats[key], b.floats[key], mix);
    this.globalUniforms[key].value = entrance ? We.lerp(entrance.floats[key], value, entranceMix) : value;
  }
  blend(this.globalUniforms.uSunDir.value, a.sunDir, b.sunDir, entrance?.sunDir);
  this.globalUniforms.uSunDir.value.normalize();
  for (const material of this.genericMaterials) {
    blend(material.uniforms.uLow.value, a.low, b.low, entrance?.low);
    blend(material.uniforms.uHigh.value, a.high, b.high, entrance?.high);
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < this.materialRecords.length; i++) {
    blend(this.materialRecords[i].material.uniforms.uLit.value, a.materials[i], b.materials[i], entrance?.materials[i]);
  }
  for (const key of Object.keys(a.sky)) blend(this.skyMaterial.uniforms[key].value, a.sky[key], b.sky[key], entrance?.sky[key]);
  const stars = We.lerp(a.stars, b.stars, mix);
  this.skyMaterial.uniforms.uStars.value = entrance ? We.lerp(entrance.stars, stars, entranceMix) : stars;
}
setCinematicTour(playing) {
  if (playing && (this.freeFlightEnabled || !this.stats.buildings)) return;
  if (!this.cinematicTour) {
    if (!playing) return;
    this.cinematicTour = this.createCinematicTour();
    this.cameraFlight = void 0;
    this.clearFlightInput();
  }
  this.cinematicTour.playing = playing;
  this.syncOrbitFromCamera();
  this.notifyCinematicTour();
}
cancelCinematicTour() {
  if (!this.cinematicTour) return;
  this.cinematicTour = null;
  this.cameraFlight = void 0;
  this.syncOrbitFromCamera();
  this.notifyCinematicTour();
}
notifyCinematicTour() {
  const state = { playing: !!this.cinematicTour?.playing, view: this.activeView, palette: this.palette };
  const signature = `${state.playing}:${state.view}:${state.palette}`;
  if (signature !== this.lastTourSignature) {
    this.lastTourSignature = signature;
    this.onTourUpdate?.(state);
  }
}
updateCinematicTour(dt) {
  const tour = this.cinematicTour;
  if (!tour?.playing || document.hidden) return;
  tour.elapsed += dt;
  const sample = sfTourSample(tour.nodes, tour.elapsed, tour.position, tour.target);
  const u = We.clamp(tour.elapsed / 4, 0, 1), entranceMix = u * u * (3 - 2 * u);
  this.camera.position.copy(tour.entrancePosition).lerp(tour.position, entranceMix);
  this.target.copy(tour.entranceTarget).lerp(tour.target, entranceMix);
  this.camera.lookAt(this.target);
  const fov = We.lerp(tour.entranceFov, 46 + 2 * Math.sin(sample.phase * Math.PI * 2), entranceMix);
  if (Math.abs(this.camera.fov - fov) > .001) {
    this.camera.fov = fov; this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  }
  const time = sfTourTimeline(sample.phase);
  this.applyTourPalette(time.from, time.to, time.mix, tour.entrancePalette, entranceMix);
  this.palette = time.label;
  document.documentElement.dataset.palette = time.label;
  this.activeView = sample.view;
  this.notifyCinematicTour();
}
